FlightReady final readiness report

A. Final verdict: INDETERMINATE

The available evidence does not establish readiness for the defined use. Complete the missing evidence or resolve the recorded conditions.

synthetic-drones.zip
Intended task: Detect drones in images.

Task compatibility: supported

The recorded labels and checked conditions support this task; model performance remains untested.

Evidence coverage: 43.0% | Critical blockers: 0

Overall index: Not scored. The frozen policy defines six geometric dimension indices, not a validated overall index. No universal readiness percentage is inferred.

C. What compromises readiness?

  1. mandatory evidence gap: Exact content overlap between development and test partitions has not been established.

    Declare split metadata and run the exact-content leakage audit on the complete partition manifest.

  2. mandatory evidence gap: Recording-group independence across partitions has not been established.

    Supply flight/session identifiers and audit their assignment across partitions.

  3. quality evidence: Independently reviewed annotation correctness: Supply an independent label audit; annotation syntax does not establish semantic correctness.

    Independently reviewed annotation correctness: Supply an independent label audit; annotation syntax does not establish semantic correctness.

    Declare split metadata and run the exact-content leakage audit on the complete partition manifest.

  2. mandatory evidence gap: Recording-group independence across partitions has not been established.

    Supply flight/session identifiers and audit their assignment across partitions.

  3. quality evidence: Independently reviewed annotation correctness: Supply an independent label audit; annotation syntax does not establish semantic correctness.

    Independently reviewed annotation correctness: Supply an independent label audit; annotation syntax does not establish semantic correctness.

All recommendations
